# Skills · A · part 22

870 entries, agent-design-patterns to agent-host-logs. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **agent-design-patterns**: 运用《智能体设计模式》(Antonio Gulli) 的 21 个模式,指导 AI Agent 系统的架构设计与选型。适用于用户在设计 Agent 工作流(选择链式/路由/并行/多Agent编排)、提升 Agent 输出质量与可靠性(反思/异常处理/Guardrails)、做 RAG 技术选型(混合检索/GraphRAG/Agentic RAG)、优化 Agent 成本与延迟(动态模型切换/回退)时使用。 ([kuhung/weread-book-skills](https://github.com/kuhung/weread-book-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-design-patterns) · ★9)
- **agent-design-patterns**: Use when you need to structure a multi-agent task (review, migration, audit, research, refactor) as a dynamic Workflow. Maps the Google Cloud agentic-AI design patterns (Sequential, Parallel, Loop, Coordinator, Hierarchical) onto Workflow primitives, points you at the matching pattern markdown, and each markdown carries a copyable Workflow template. Trigger on "which agent pattern", "orchestrate this with subagents", "fan out", "multi-agent workflow", "pipeline this", "run a workflow for". ([csmedeiros/agent-design-patterns](https://github.com/csmedeiros/agent-design-patterns/tree/HEAD/skills/agent-design-patterns) · ★1)
- **agent-design-patterns**: Seven proven archetypes for subagent design (Explorer, Builder, Surgeon, Orchestrator, Specialist, Guardian, Connector). Injected into the architect agent to guide agent roster design decisions — which pattern to use, when to merge domains, and how to compose agents for common project shapes. ([UsernameTron/Claude-Project-Structure-Assistant](https://github.com/UsernameTron/Claude-Project-Structure-Assistant/tree/HEAD/subagent-lifecycle/skills/agent-design-patterns) · 2 copies)
